Use for the build and revise phases. ([ShyamSundhar1411/claude-plugins](https://github.com/ShyamSundhar1411/claude-plugins/blob/HEAD/plugins/crucible/agents/crucible-hand.md) · ★1)
- **crucible-judge**: Deliberately blind independent grader for crucible studies. Receives ONLY a success criterion and anonymized sample outputs — no arm identity, no hypothesis, no metric name, no idea which output is the "treatment". Scores each item against the criterion and returns strict JSON. MUST BE USED for the `crucible judge` step. This is Gate 2 — the check on an agent grading its own work. ([nagisanzenin/crucible](https://github.com/nagisanzenin/crucible/blob/HEAD/agents/crucible-judge.md))
- **crucible-maintainer**: Maintainer agent for the Crucible project (regression CI for Claude Code configs). Use for triaging issues, implementing roadmap items, reviewing scenario contributions, cutting releases, and keeping README/docs in sync. Commits and publishes as Khalid Vance. ([klashkaan-cmyk/crucible](https://github.com/klashkaan-cmyk/crucible/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/crucible-maintainer.md) · ★2)
- **crucible-qg-fix**: Fix agent / Plan Writer for Crucible quality-gate — applies fixes for red-team findings (main fix loop, re-reviewed each round) and the post-pass minor quick-fix. - **crucible-qg-judge**: Stagnation judge for Crucible quality-gate — mechanical cross-round comparison of finding sets. Pinned to Sonnet (cheap mechanical check). - **crucible-qg-verifier**: Mechanical structural checker for Crucible quality-gate — fix verification, the persistence checker, and the fix-generated-defect checker. - **crucible-red-team**: Adversarial reviewer (Devil's Advocate) for Crucible quality-gate and red-team. Pinned to Opus because recall-critical adversarial review degrades on weaker models. - **crunchflow-judge**: CrunchFlow stage 3 — scores the three competing designs, merges the best into docs/02-spec.md, and runs the spec linter to produce a SPEC SCORE. Cannot write code. ([ahamedumar15/crunchflow](https://github.com/ahamedumar15/crunchflow/blob/HEAD/crunchflow/agents/judge.md))
- **crunchflow-planner**: CrunchFlow stage 4 — shards the approved spec into small tasks with test criteria and an ALLOWED FILES list per task (the scope guard enforces this list). Cannot write code. - **crunchflow-qa**: CrunchFlow stage 6 — adversarial QA that trusts nobody. ([ahamedumar15/crunchflow](https://github.com/ahamedumar15/crunchflow/blob/HEAD/crunchflow/agents/qa.md))
- **crunchflow-release**: CrunchFlow stage 7 — generates the Mission Control dashboard and the final run summary with requirement→task→code→test traceability. Cannot change code, tests, or the spec.
